# Known Physical Bitcoin Attacks
A list of known attacks against Bitcoin / crypto asset owning entities that occurred in meatspace. NOTE: this list is not comprehensive; many attacks are not publicly reported. If you are aware of an attack that is not listed, please open an issue or pull request — see [CONTRIBUTING.md](./CONTRIBUTING.md) for what qualifies and how to add entries.

For interactive visual dashboards of this data, check out [our map](https://jlopp.github.io/physical-bitcoin-attacks/) and the [Crypto Crime Dashboard](https://cryptocrime-dashboard.vercel.app/)
For more comprehensive statistical breakdowns of attack attributes, check out [gart.io](https://gart.io/stats)
Another comprehensive list of attacks is available at [bitcoin-wrench-attacks.zaup.org](https://bitcoin-wrench-attacks.zaup.org/index.html)

For high level analyses and best practices, readers may be interested in this 2020 presentation: ["The Hodlguard- a primer on physical security in Bitcoin"](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HUgPhPkS2yc) and [slides](https://prezi.com/view/P46hHguLiPvqA7aZ7gYR).
Also, Jameson's [2025 presentation](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bxjKAtxy6J4) with updated metrics and advice.
Finally, [this academic paper](./Wrench_Attacks_Research.pdf) analyzing physical attacks using Bitcointalk as a data source.

Testifying at the federal trial of Remy Ra St Felix (North Carolina, 2024), Seemungal confirmed under oath that he had been "home invaded" and robbed of all his crypto at gunpoint; he said that this robbery, and one suffered by his accomplice "Tidy"/"Neb", led him in summer 2022 to set up the crypto home-invasion network he ran with St Felix.](https://archive.is/Iddsu) [(original link)](https://storage.courtlistener.com/recap/gov.uscourts.ncmd.96201/gov.uscourts.ncmd.96201.115.0.pdf) |

| March 10 2023 | 62 y/o Chinese businessman & interpreter | Bangkok, Thailand | [Victims were abducted in Bangkok by five men dressed as police officers — actually immigration bureau personnel — and driven around for over four hours under threat of prosecution, with 60,000 USDT demanded; the victim's son negotiated and paid 30,000 USDT before they were released. Three immigration officers were arrested, one remains at large, and dismissal proceedings were initiated.](https://archive.is/7zPUZ) [(original link)](https://www.hk01.com/%E5%8D%B3%E6%99%82%E4%B8%AD%E5%9C%8B/880329/) |

| April 2023 | Unidentified Woman | Dubai, United Arab Emirates | [Driver & 3 friends lured an investor into a fake deal, robbed, electrocuted, stripped naked, and took blackmail videos.](https://archive.is/geUM5) [(original link)](https://www.khaleejtimes.com/uae/crime/dubai-4-expats-jailed-for-robbing-woman-of-dh42000-using-cash-to-buy-bitcoin)|
| April 2023 | Unidentified man | Turin, Italy | [Two men aged 43 and 47 showed up at a Turin financial intermediary's business demanding the return of a USB drive they claimed held one million euros in bitcoins, though the victim had no connection to it, issuing death threats and against his family and business, with a fire breaking out at the premises. After his complaint the Rivoli carabinieri placed them under house arrest on June 22 2023 by order of the Turin court for aggravated attempted extortion.](https://archive.is/bBECh) [(original link)](https://www.ansa.it/piemonte/notizie/2023/06/22/volevano-un-milione-di-euro-in-bitcoin-arrestati-per-estorsione_b4700e8d-ab45-4a2b-8be1-037878db1064.html) |
| April 2023 | Three brothers | Milan, Italy | [Three young brothers were assaulted and held captive in a Milan bed and breakfast by three Sardinian men claiming to have been defrauded by them, with the kidnappers demanding EUR 120,000 in cryptocurrency plus the return of valuable watches. Geolocation of the ransom calls let the carabinieri free the victims and arrest the perpetrators, who were definitively sentenced to 9 years and 1 day.](https://archive.is/vmGTg) [(original link)](https://www.wikimilano.it/desk/news/arrestati-in-sardegna-tre-uomini-condannati-per-sequestro-a-milano-volevano-120mila-euro-di-riscatto/) |
| April 2023 | Three Dominican tourists | Medellin, Colombia | [Two women contacted three Dominican tourists via dating apps and lured them to an El Poblado nightclub in Medellín, administering scopolamine; the victims were taken to a rented apartment where their passwords were extracted and accounts emptied over 16 hours — 21 purchases totaling 11 million pesos plus about 43 million pesos in Bitcoin and Ethereum. The two women were captured, accepted the charges, and the Fiscalía created a judicial wallet to return the frozen crypto.](https://archive.is/rahno) [(original link)](https://www.eltiempo.com/colombia/medellin/medellin-red-escopolamina-a-turistas-en-parque-lleras-para-robarles-las-cuentas-785151) |
| April 2023 | Several men | Birmingham, England | [For ten months a gang of five men used fake Grindr profiles to lure LGBT+ men to meetings in Birmingham and Derby, assaulting them, threatening them with knives and using their phones to empty their accounts, for total damages around GBP 100,000; one victim was drugged and forced to unlock his phone, his wallets emptied through more than 80 transactions. On January 13 2025 Birmingham Crown Court sentenced the five men to prison terms of up to 17 years and 3 months.](https://archive.is/9nh4x) [(original link)](https://www.bbc.co.uk/news/articles/cpq9vey1ndxo) |
| April 12 2023 | 76 y/o couple | Durham, North Carolina, United States | [2 men posing as construction workers invade home, force transfer of $250K in crypto.](https://archive.is/Vp5J6) [(original link)](https://www.wral.com/story/durham-couple-forced-to-wire-150k-of-cryptocurrency-at-gunpoint-during-home-invasion-police-investigating/20819450/)|
| April 15 2023 | Ronaldo Esteban Peralta Bogado | Presidente Franco, Alto Parana, Paraguay | [Between 8 and 10 armed men broke into the Arimar mining farm at Km 11 Monday in Presidente Franco around 2 a.m. and overpowered the 18-year-old guard, spending about two hours dismantling and loading 492 ASIC miners, plus a truck, laptops, phones, 100,000 pesos and the surveillance DVR. No arrests had been reported at publication.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260422021341/https://www.abc.com.py/este/2023/04/15/roban-casi-500-aparatos-de-mineradoras-de-criptomonedas-en-pdte-franco/) [(original link)](https://www.abc.com.py/este/2023/04/15/roban-casi-500-aparatos-de-mineradoras-de-criptomonedas-en-pdte-franco/) |
| April 17 2023 | C | Anyang, Gyeonggi, South Korea | [Two men broke into the Anyang home of a man running a crypto trading 'leading room,' taped and held him captive, demanding the return of invested funds — the main suspect claiming to have lost KRW 5 billion on his advice — and forcing him to ingest zolpidem; the safe was not opened and nothing was taken. The victim alerted a neighbor, police intervened, and the Anyang court issued detention warrants for aggravated robbery and unlawful confinement.](https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/421/0006760786) |
| May 2023 | Two couriers for a crypto company | Anapa, Russia | [A Muscovite and two accomplices posed as police officers to stage fake arrests: they abducted and held captive a courier for a cryptocurrency company in Anapa, then a second one in Moscow whose car they also stole, beating them, threatening to kill them and confronting them with false accusations of fraud and terrorism financing. They demanded RUB 5 million per courier from their families and RUB 25 million from the company's director to 'avoid prosecution.' Sentenced by the Anapa court to 10 years in a strict-regime penal colony and a RUB 140,000 fine (kidnapping, extortion of a very large amount, robbery, vehicle theft), the organizer had his sentence upheld on cassation appeal on August 11, 2026; the cases of the two accomplices were separated.](https://archive.is/TaXMM) [(original link)](https://www.kuban.kp.ru/online/news/7116890/) |

| June 2023 | Unidentified family | L'Ile-Bizard, Montreal, Canada | [Three masked men overpowered a man leaving his home in L'Ile-Bizard and forced him at knifepoint to transfer his bitcoins via a Binance QR code — which he made fail — while his partner was bound at gunpoint and their infant torn from her arms; the man alerted 911 via his smartwatch and both victims eventually escaped. Joe-Marshall Altidort, 22, pleaded guilty and was sentenced on February 10 2026 to 10 years in a penitentiary; his accomplices were never arrested.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260211051500/https://www.lapresse.ca/actualites/justice-et-faits-divers/2026-02-10/couple-ligote-lors-d-un-vol-de-bitcoins/un-cauchemar-tout-droit-sorti-d-un-film-dit-la-juge.php) [(original link)](https://www.lapresse.ca/actualites/justice-et-faits-divers/2026-02-10/couple-ligote-lors-d-un-vol-de-bitcoins/un-cauchemar-tout-droit-sorti-d-un-film-dit-la-juge.php) |
| June 5 2023 | Unidentified man | Kaohsiung, Taiwan | [Kuo Che-an set up an ambush on Instagram with three accomplices, posing as a buyer of 20,000 USDT and luring the seller to a Kaohsiung apartment; there he beat the victim with a baseball bat while others pepper-sprayed him and produced a knife, forcing him to hand over wallet credentials from which 20,000 USDT were transferred, 18,000 resold and split. The Kaohsiung District Court sentenced the perpetrators on March 31 2026 to terms up to 8 years for armed robbery by a group of three or more, with a suspended sentence for the accomplice who compensated the victim.](https://archive.is/pCbsO) [(original link)](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=KSDM%2C113%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C49%2C20260331%2C1) |
| June 12 2023 | Kayla Pawa | Birkenhead, Auckland, New Zealand | [Kayla Pawa, 28, was abducted from her bedroom by armed intruders linked to the Nomads gang and held across two Auckland locations for 24 days, during which she was tied up, beaten with a bat and hammer, burned with a blowtorch, threatened with finger amputation and forced to dig her own grave — all to break her will into revealing a crypto password she insisted she did not know. She escaped from a vehicle on July 6 2023 and flagged down an ambulance.](https://archive.is/NTGXT) [(original link)](https://www.nzherald.co.nz/nz/crime/auckland-kidnapping-bernadette-rawiri-jailed-womans-torture-over-cryptocurrency-password/VW6FHED2MJCTJLTFECEVQFVUWE/) |
| June 22 2023 | Mr. Liu | Taipei, Taiwan | [Two men posing as crypto buyers lured trader Liu to a Neihu meeting in Taipei, where Lin overpowered him with a machete, blindfolded and zip-tied him while Yang used his phone to impersonate him: 84 then 21,000 USDT were transferred plus TWD 10,000 cash taken, before the pair fled to split the loot at a KTV. The Shilin District Court sentenced them on May 21 2025 to 7 years and 8 months for aggravated robbery.](https://www.chinatimes.com/realtimenews/20250521003418-260402) |
| July 2023 | Crypto entreprenuer | Rudny, Yekaterinburg, Russia | [A four-man gang from Altai Krai — tipped off by a police economic-security head who sold them Interior Ministry data for RUB 300,000 — kidnapped a cryptocurrency entrepreneur in Rudny after weeks of surveillance, beating him and threatening him with firearms until he surrendered wallet logins; over RUB 5.6–5.8 million in crypto were transferred and resold. Tried by jury, the defendants received 7 to 14 years in a strict-regime colony plus fines in November 2025, including 10 years for the former officer.](https://archive.is/Jw7sF) [(original link)](http://rapsinews.ru/judicial_news/20251121/311363444.html) |
| July 5 2023 | Two Turkish nationals | Medellin, Colombia | [Two Turkish nationals contacted through a dating app were taken to a Medellín apartment and made to drink a substance leaving them unconscious for two days; the attackers then transferred more than 165 million pesos in crypto plus 19 million in goods. | July 5 2023 | Yeh Chia-jung | Tainan, Taiwan | [A group formed on Telegram by an organizer nicknamed 'James' and led by Chung Jui-chien (鍾睿謙) bought a car without registration papers and three air pistols the same day, then lured Yeh Chia-jung into a fake exchange of USDT 60,000 for TWD 1,900,000 in front of the Carrefour on Zhongshan Road in Rende, Tainan, on July 5, 2023 at 10:37 p.m. Once Yeh got into the back of the vehicle, two men held him at gunpoint, struck his left cheek with the butt of an air rifle, and forced him to hand over his phone passcode, which they used to send his associate a fake cash receipt confirmation, triggering the transfer of the USDT 60,000 to a designated wallet; Yeh was released at an intersection in Rende and his iPhone was thrown out of the window during the getaway.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TNDM%2C113%2C%E5%8E%9F%E8%A8%B4%2C15%2C20250527%2C2) |
| July 7 2023 | Chien Huan-ju | New Taipei, Taiwan | [A Telegram organizer nicknamed 'Doctor Strange' recruited two accomplices for a fake purchase of 95,000 USDT (~TWD 3,035,250) in Sanchong; the seller was locked in the car, choked and immobilized, and her iPhone containing the wallet torn away. She refused to give the passcode and escaped with wounds to wrist and knee; the phone was resold the next day for TWD 8,500.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=PCDM%2C113%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C362%2C20250227%2C1) |

The incident was part of a nine-robbery conspiracy against gay and bisexual men lured via fake Grindr profiles, which drew sentences of 12 years to 17 years 3 months on January 13 2025.](https://caselaw.nationalarchives.gov.uk/ewca/crim/2026/92) |
| July 20 2023 | 56 y/o woman | Élancourt, France | [A man disguised as a delivery courier and two accomplices forced their way into a woman's home, taping her from feet to shoulders and threatening her with a machete and hammer to extort 10 million euros in crypto from her son, a New York-based crypto entrepreneur; only 30,000 euros were actually paid. She freed herself from the gag and screamed, and a neighbor climbed in through a window and freed her.](https://www.franceinfo.fr/economie/bitcoin/cryptomonnaies-proces-aux-assises-requis-contre-un-commando-soupconne-d-avoir-mene-plusieurs-enlevements_7757873.html) |
| July 27 2023 | Kevin Butler | East Meadow, New York State, United States | [Remy Ra St Felix, head of the home invasion team of the Florida-based Seemungal network, traveled from Florida to Long Island, New York, to break into the home of a family of five in East Meadow: he had identified his target, Kevin Butler, a cryptocurrency holder, had his address, photos of his house, and his driver's license, was surveilling the home, and was discussing on Telegram with the accomplice 'Neb' a plan to access the victim's Coinbase account by using his child as 'leverage.' He was arrested by law enforcement on the morning of July 27, 2023, alone in his vehicle in the parking lot of a McDonald's in West Hempstead, before carrying out the act: police seized a pistol at his feet, an AR-style rifle, and plastic zip ties in the trunk. On September 11, 2024, he was sentenced to 47 years in prison.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260524233442/https://www.justice.gov/archives/opa/pr/twelve-defendants-sentenced-violent-home-invasion-robberies-steal-cryptocurrency) [(original link)](https://www.justice.gov/archives/opa/pr/twelve-defendants-sentenced-violent-home-invasion-robberies-steal-cryptocurrency) |

| December 25 2023 | 4 mining employees | Hernandarias, Alto Paraná, Paraguay | [Around twelve armed men arriving in a medium-sized truck broke into a cryptocurrency mining warehouse in the San Francisco neighborhood of Hernandarias, a border town near Ciudad del Este, and overpowered the four employees present. They made off with approximately 430 mining machines, as well as three rifles belonging to the site's security guards.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260617221107/https://www.h2foz.com.br/fronteira/assalto-mineradora-criptomoedas-paraguai/) [(original link)](https://www.h2foz.com.br/fronteira/assalto-mineradora-criptomoedas-paraguai/) |
| 2024 | Unidentified man | Hebei, China | [An OTC USDT trader meeting familiar buyers at a hotel had his 400,000 USDT transferred and cash seized; ten people identified through conversations, withdrawal records and CCTV were convicted of robbery with violence and sentenced to 8 to over 10 years, with USDT 53,262 seized restored. Neither the date nor jurisdiction was published.](https://zhuanlan.zhihu.com/p/2041845370418880567) |
| 2024 | Two Brothers | Zaporizhzhia, Ukraine | [In summer 2024 a six-man organized group surveilled a crypto operator's bank withdrawals, then beat him and his brother — heads enclosed in plastic bags — and took them to a suburban house demanding a fabricated UAH 600,000 debt; when unpaid, they raided a partner's home for over UAH 157,000 in cash, jewelry and equipment before abandoning the brothers. Six members were arrested by KORD on September 25 2024.](https://www.061.ua/ru/news/3837117/v-zaporoze-zaderzali-clenov-gruppirovki-pohitivsih-i-trebovavsih-600-tysac-griven-u-kriptovalutcika) |
| January 2024 | Tega Abikure | Abuja, Nigeria | [Tega Abikure, founder of crypto company Kumo Africa, was abducted mid-Abuja in January 2024 and recounted being beaten, tortured and humiliated during captivity, without detailing his release. Recorded fragments suggest the kidnapping was linked to a failed OTC bitcoin transaction eight months earlier in the United States; rivals allegedly coveted his large crypto portfolio.](https://web.archive.org/web/20240214051656/https://leadership.ng/i-was-tortured-by-kidnappers-crypto-boss/) [(original link)](https://leadership.ng/i-was-tortured-by-kidnappers-crypto-boss/) |
| January 3 2024 | 29 y/o man | Anavyssos, Attica, Greece | [A programmer parked outside a friend's home in Anavyssos was restrained by men posing as police officers, taken in his own car, and locked in another vehicle's trunk in a wooded area, where violence and threats extracted his phones, cards and PINs and forced fingerprint-unlock of his crypto app for EUR 3,000 in transfers. Bound, handcuffed and sprayed with bleach, he freed himself and alerted police; a 25-year-old Moldovan was arrested on the spot.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250613233338/https://www.protothema.gr/greece/article/1452623/anavussos-pos-egine-i-arpagi-tou-programmatisti-ton-edesa-me-heiropedes-kai-ton-psekasan-me-hlorini/) [(original link)](https://www.protothema.gr/greece/article/1452623/anavussos-pos-egine-i-arpagi-tou-programmatisti-ton-edesa-me-heiropedes-kai-ton-psekasan-me-hlorini/) |
| January 8 2024 | 17 y/o man | Gargas, France | [A high school student was abducted on his way to school in Gargas (Vaucluse) by individuals who were actually looking for another young man from the town, known to them only by his online pseudonym, in order to extort a cryptocurrency security key giving access to several tens of thousands of euros. Realizing their mistake, the kidnappers released the teenager, who filed a complaint with the Apt gendarmerie.](https://france3-regions.franceinfo.fr/provence-alpes-cote-d-azur/vaucluse/un-lyceen-est-enleve-par-erreur-a-gargas-ses-ravisseurs-finissent-par-le-liberer-2907878.html) |
| January 16 2024 | 42 y/o man | Cluj, Romania | [Restaurant owner kidnapped, doused in diesel, force fed alcohol, has finger cut off, until he transferred $200K USD in crypto.](https://archive.is/eqj4y) [(original link)](https://observatornews.ro/eveniment/celebru-om-de-afaceri-din-cluj-rapit-si-torturat-pentru-codul-de-la-criptomonede-talharii-iau-taiat-un-deget-558888.html)|
| January 18 2024 | Roberto Felipe Mejía Caicedo | Envigado, Antioquia, Colombia | [Roberto Felipe Mejía Caicedo, 43, a Cali influencer, was lured to a shopping center on the Medellín–Envigado border under the pretext of a crypto deal and abducted to a country house, held about 36 hours by members of 'La Oficina' who demanded 3,000 million pesos and forced him to transfer USD 184,000 in crypto before he alerted his bodyguard by WhatsApp. CTI and army Gaula units located the house, freed him unharmed and arrested five kidnappers on the spot.](https://www.infobae.com/colombia/2024/01/20/cti-libero-a-comerciante-secuestrado-en-medellin-durante-una-negociacion-de-criptomonedas/) |
| January 19 2024 | The Dubliner Irish Pub | Bucharest, Romania | [At around 3:30 a.m., two men stole a bitcoin ATM terminal from a pub. According to the capital's police, they had rented a vehicle abroad, bought tools specifically for breaking into and carrying off the ATM, and had scouted the location beforehand, then destroyed a surveillance camera during the operation; the damage is estimated at RON 87,500, including over RON 85,000 in cash contained in the machine.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250912190521/https://www.gandul.ro/actualitate/barbatii-furat-bancomat-bitcoin-restaurant-capitala-prinsi-20157421) [(original link)](https://www.gandul.ro/actualitate/barbatii-furat-bancomat-bitcoin-restaurant-capitala-prinsi-20157421) |
| January 28 2024 | Unidentified man | Leipzig, Germany | [Three men recruited by a former business partner who knew the victim held at least a five-figure crypto sum waited in his Leipzig stairwell; one, armed with a machete, forced the door, struck him and held the blade to his throat demanding his laptop and e-wallet password, while an accomplice pinned his partner to the wall. The victim resisted and shouted, suffering deep hand cuts from a glass table; the attackers fled without taking anything.](https://www.hrr-strafrecht.de/5/25/5-205-25.php) |
| January 31 2024 | Belarusian couple | Phuket, Thailand | [5 Russians arrested in crypto abduction case (~USD 801,200)](https://archive.is/IcEmQ) [(original link)](https://www.bangkokpost.com/thailand/general/2735294/5-russians-arrested-in-crypto-abduction-case)|
| February 2024 | Unidentified | Winnsboro, Texas, United States | ["GothFerrari" broke into victim's home and stole hardware wallet with ~100 BTC ($5M+); sentenced to 78 months under RICO conspiracy as part of $250M crypto theft ring.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260528120832/https://www.justice.gov/usao-dc/pr/gothferrari-sentenced-78-months-prison-role-massive-cryptocurrency-heist) [(original link)](https://www.justice.gov/usao-dc/pr/gothferrari-sentenced-78-months-prison-role-massive-cryptocurrency-heist)|
| February 18 2024 | Unidentified man | Têche, France | [During the night of 18 to 19 February 2024 in Têche (Isère), a driver who had just sold a quad bike for cash was stopped on the road by fake police officers driving a vehicle fitted with a flashing light and wearing jackets marked "Police". Believing it was a traffic stop, he let them approach; the criminals handcuffed him, put a bag over his head and held him for about four hours, until around 3 a.m., forcing him to transfer EUR 75,000 in cryptocurrency before releasing him.](https://www.franceinfo.fr/faits-divers/enlevements/info-franceinfo-trois-hommes-accuses-d-avoir-sequestre-et-derobe-75-000-euros-en-cryptomonnaies-a-leur-victime-presentes-a-un-juge-en-isere-en-vue-de-leur-mise-en-examen_7501612.html) |
| February 26 2024 | Abu Bakkar Siddique | Chattogram, Bangladesh | [About USD 272,000 from Binance (about BDT 3 to 3.1 crore) + BDT 10 lakh — On the night of 26 February 2024, a Detective Branch team (North-South zone) of the Chattogram Metropolitan Police led by Inspector Ruhul Amin detained freelancer Abu Bakkar Siddique and a friend in the Bayezid area, seized their phones, threatened them with a false cybercrime and money-laundering case, then took them to the DB office in Mansurabad. In custody, the officers forcibly unlocked his phone with his fingerprint, logged into his Binance account and transferred about USD 272,000 to another account, and took BDT 10 lakh from his bank account.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250504132251/https://www.thedailystar.net/news/bangladesh/crime-justice/news/looting-tk-31cr-freelancer-db-custody-6-cops-suspended-3566611) [(original link)](https://www.thedailystar.net/news/bangladesh/crime-justice/news/looting-tk-31cr-freelancer-db-custody-6-cops-suspended-3566611) |
| February 29 2024 | Two victims | Wellington, New Zealand | [In two Wellington supermarket car park robberies of P2P crypto traders (February 29 and March 5 2024), the attacker struck one victim repeatedly in the neck and pointed a handgun-like object at the second's head, taking about NZD 7,000 per victim (approx. NZD 14,000 total). Hiram Kanona St John Tufuga pleaded guilty to two counts of aggravated robbery and was sentenced on July 4 2025 to three years ten months, with NZD 2,000 reparations per victim.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250916002446/https://www.police.govt.nz/news/release/information-sought-following-aggravated-robberies-wellington) [(original link)](https://www.police.govt.nz/news/release/information-sought-following-aggravated-robberies-wellington) |

| March 7 2024 | Mr. Kwong | Hung Hom, Kowloon, Hong Kong | [Mr. Kwong, 40, had withdrawn HKD 1,000,000 in cash on behalf of his employer to buy discounted USDT in a Hung Hom industrial building; one man greeted him, then four others burst in brandishing meat cleavers, the first suspect counted and took the entire sum, and the five masked men fled. The Kowloon City district crime squad arrested the unit's 56-year-old tenant that same day for robbery.](https://www.hk01.com/article/998146) |
| March 10 2024 | Young Couple | Montreal, Quebec, Canada | [Gang of 4 kidnaps couple and robs them of $25,000 in cryptocurrency.](https://archive.is/tZB6N) [(original link)](https://www.cbc.ca/news/canada/montreal/suspects-arrested-montreal-calgary-crypto-kidnapping-1.7203455)|
| March 12 2024 | Two women | Rijswijk, Netherlands | [Two masked men who arranged a meeting through kinky.nl broke into a Rijswijk home, threatened the two women residents with firearms, taped and held them captive for hours; they took EUR 7,500 cash, crypto, and cards, forced facial-recognition transfer of EUR 26,001 to a Bitvavo account, and withdrew EUR 10,000 from ATMs (total approx. EUR 43,501). A man later received six years in prison for the violent crypto robbery.](https://uitspraken.rechtspraak.nl/details?id=ECLI:NL:RBDHA:2026:18579) |
| March 13 2024 | Sunoco gas station | Birmingham, Alabama, United States | [Three men robbed a Birmingham, Alabama Sunoco gas station around 2:40 a.m., taking a Bitcoin ATM and firing shots at a man, who was uninjured. Police released suspect images and appealed for witnesses; no arrests had been announced by then.](https://www.wbrc.com/2024/04/02/birmingham-police-searching-suspects-sunoco-gas-station-robbery/) |
| March 15 2024 | ___ Yevgini | Samui Island, Thailand | [Gang of 6 Russians robs Russian man and wife at coffee shop for 1.8 million baht in BTC.](https://archive.is/EnzIt)|
| March 21 2024 | Man in his forties | Seoul, South Korea | [KRW 100,000,000 — On March 21, 2024, at around 0:45 a.m., about ten men in their twenties, friends or classmates who had split up roles (luring, theft, getaway, blocking pursuers), arranged via Telegram to meet a man in his forties in Yeoksam-dong to sell him Tether below market price. While pretending to transfer the coins from a phone and then to count the money, they restrained the victim, beat him, and fled with a paper bag containing 100 million won; they in fact held no cryptocurrency at all and had obtained the victim's information via Telegram.](https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/001/0014639794) |
| March 22 2024 | Li Kuo-hsiang | Taichung, Xitun District, Taiwan | [Tsai Chia-yu set up a fake sale to buyer Li Kuo-hsiang of TWD 6,420,000 in crypto at a Taichung studio with two accomplices and pepper spray; on the signal an accomplice overturned the table to grab the cash bags, the victims resisted and the money spilled, and Li was injured in the struggle. Li and a friend pinned one attacker until police arrived; Tsai fled but was arrested hours later, along with the driver.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TCDM%2C113%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C755%2C20241211%2C5) |
| April 2024 | An adult man | Tehran, Iran | [A man from out of town was abducted outside a Tehran hospital by three young men who beat him and forced him into a car, then called his wife threatening death and demanded IRR 500 billion in Tether over an unpaid IRR 50 billion USDT debt. Sensing police were near, they released the hostage unharmed without receiving anything and fled.](https://www.hamshahrionline.ir/news/848163/%D8%A2%D8%AF%D9%85-%D8%B1%D8%A8%D8%A7%DB%8C%DB%8C-%D8%A8%D8%B1%D8%A7%DB%8C-%DA%AF%D8%B1%D9%81%D8%AA%D9%86-%DB%B5%DB%B0-%D9%85%DB%8C%D9%84%DB%8C%D8%A7%D8%B1%D8%AF-%D8%AA%D8%AA%D8%B1) |
| April 2024 | 40 y/o man | New York, United States | [A man went to a lower Manhattan hotel for a cash-for-crypto exchange and was lured to a room where two accomplices waited; the three beat him, taped his wrists, ankles and mouth, and fled with USD 20,000 in cash. The NYPD released surveillance footage of the three suspects, aged 18 to 22.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250726093804/https://www.foxbusiness.com/markets/nyc-police-search-suspects-cash-crypto-deal-bad) [(original link)](https://www.foxbusiness.com/markets/nyc-police-search-suspects-cash-crypto-deal-bad) |
| April 1 2024 | Liao | Taichung, Dali District, Taiwan | [A heavily indebted USDT trader, Lin, paid a friend TWD 100,000 to rob his regular client Liao during a fake sale of TWD 3 million in USDT in Dali (Taichung); once Liao boarded with TWD 3,303,000 in cash, Chen strangled him from the back seat with kraft paper simulating a weapon and took the bag. Both were arrested within nine hours, the money returned, and each sentenced to 3 years 6 months for aggravated robbery.](https://judgment.judicial.gov.tw/FJUD/data.aspx?ty=JD&id=TCDM%2C113%2C%E8%A8%B4%2C708%2C20241009%2C2) |
| April 2 2024 | 36 y/o man | Limassol, Cyprus | [A 36-year-old Russian national meeting a stranger in Limassol to exchange EUR 50,000 for crypto was grabbed by the throat in his car while two other foreign nationals seized the bag of cash, all three fleeing. Limassol CID opened an investigation and a judicial arrest warrant was issued against a 35-year-old, the two accomplices remaining unidentified.](https://cyprus-mail.com/2024/04/03/man-robbed-of-50000-euros-is-cryptocurrency-deal-gone-wrong/) |
| April 2 2024 | Mr. Lau | Hung Hom, Kowloon, Hong Kong | [A 19-year-old crypto trader was summoned to a Hung Hom hotel room he shared and held captive and beaten by six men and two women demanding HKD 180,000 in trading profits, struck with a softball bat before alerting relatives by phone. Police arrested three men including his roommate and a 17-year-old for unlawful confinement, extortion and causing bodily harm; five other participants fled.](https://www.hk01.com/article/1006320) |
| April 10 2024 | Mr. Chen | Chiayi, West District, Taiwan | [In Chiayi, a gang of eight staged a fake USDT sale: trader Mr. Chen handed TWD 2.36 million to Lou for counting when a minor accomplice pepper-sprayed him and the driver sped off with fake plates; Chen clung to the windshield until Lou ordered acceleration and swerving, throwing him to his death. In February 2025 the Chiayi court sentenced Lou to 13 years and Qiu to 11 years 2 months for robbery causing death, and six accomplices to 7 months to 3 years; the money had been returned to the family.](https://web.archive.org/web/20251013030947/https://www.cna.com.tw/news/asoc/202502210268.aspx) [(original link)](https://www.cna.com.tw/news/asoc/202502210268.aspx) |
| April 11 2024 | Man in his thirties | Seoul, South Korea | [In a Gangnam officetel, a man lured a buyer promising below-market USDT then struck him repeatedly on the head with a blunt object and fled with the KRW 500 million cash brought for the deal. Arrested the next day and charged with attempted murder in aggravated robbery, with two getaway accomplices also arrested; most of the cash was recovered, and Gangnam police linked the case to a series of at least four OTC USDT robberies.](https://n.news.naver.com/mnews/article/014/0005174139) |
| April 17 2024 | Crypto Entrepreneur | Bonnyrigg, Midlothian, Scotland | [Robert Barr, 28, an inmate at Edinburgh Prison, organized attacks on cryptocurrency holders from his cell: after a January 2024 burglary of a Bonnyrigg crypto start-up co-founder's home netted a Ledger wallet but no PIN, two masked men came on April 17 2024 to seize the crypto assets but gave up when faced with the video doorbell. No crypto was obtained; Barr's estimated target holdings exceeded GBP 2 million.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260313173254/https://news.stv.tv/west-central/pair-accused-of-planning-robberies-to-steal-17m-in-cryptocurrency) [(original link)](https://news.stv.tv/west-central/pair-accused-of-planning-robberies-to-steal-17m-in-cryptocurrency) |
| April 18 2024 | 11 Traders | Lianhe Zaobao, Singapore | [4 men rob suspected Chinese crypto gambling ring of $3M USD.](https://archive.is/ZW6GW)|
| April 22 2024 | Mining Farm | Minga Guazú, Alto Paraná, Paraguay | [A gang of about fifteen attacked a Minga Guazú mining farm and made off with 362 bitcoin mining machines; police arrested a suspected fence reselling miners and seized 15 more machines in a search, recovering 21 total, while the robbers remained at large.](https://www.lanacion.com.py/pais/2024/05/03/detuvieron-a-una-mujer-y-recuperaron-21-maquinas-en-caso-de-robo-a-criptomineria/) |
| April 27 2024 | Unidentified | Port Moody, British Columbia, Canada | [Violent home invasion, suspect arrested at airport and eventually sentenced to 7 years.](https://archive.is/UOFBy) [(original link)](https://www.msn.com/en-ca/news/canada/b-c-family-tortured-for-13-hours-by-thieves-impersonating-mailmen-in-2m-crypto-heist/ar-AA1QU0ko)|
| May 2024 | Unidentified man | Kyiv, Ukraine | [Four residents of Kyiv and the Kyiv and Cherkasy oblasts, posing as law enforcement officers, targeted a 20-year-old online business founder after estimating his income, falsely accused him of collaborating with Russia, and threatened him with lengthy detention for high treason, offering to "settle the matter" for USD 250,000. The victim transferred 250,000 USDT (over UAH 10 million), which the extortionists immediately converted to cash; the four suspects were identified and then arrested during simultaneous searches, and were notified of suspicion under Article 189, Part 4 of the Criminal Code (extortion, up to 12 years).](https://cyberpolice.gov.ua/news/vymanyly-u-cholovika--tysyach-dolariv-u-kryptovalyuti-policzejski-vykryly-zlochynnu-grupu-vymagachiv-5925/) |
| May 5 2024 | Quentin Cepeljac | London, England | [Belgian barber Quentin Cepeljac bragged about being a successful crypto dealer to impress a woman, who invited him to her luxury flat — a trap where he was attacked with a wrench; he turned out to hold only £6.71 in crypto. The attackers were quickly caught because they had rented an Airbnb for the trap.](https://archive.is/EBmqW) |
| May 7 2024 | Mrs. Chu | Sheung Shui, New Territories, Hong Kong | [Four men blocked Mrs. Chu's car in Sheung Shui, forcibly taking her while beating her husband with fists and a stun gun, then ordered her to settle a HKD 15 million debt linked to her crypto investments before releasing her near Ta Kwu Ling; she went straight to police. | May 9 2024 | Sofian F | Pibrac, France | [Six masked armed men blocked Sofian F., described as a drug trafficker, and a friend selling a car in Pibrac, abducting them; the friend was released hours later but Sofian was held two days in Toulouse-suburb hideouts, beaten and tortured into surrendering about EUR 60,000 in bitcoin, EUR 100,000 cash, a Rolex and other items (~EUR 200,000 total). The alleged organizer reportedly targeted him over a EUR 15,000 drug debt because of his reputation for wealth.](https://france3-regions.franceinfo.fr/occitanie/haute-garonne/toulouse/kidnapping-pres-de-toulouse-cinq-personnes-jugees-pour-l-enlevement-d-un-homme-et-l-extorsion-de-200-000-euros-3349948.html) |

Harare police freed the couple on May 19 after relatives passed their location; Moses Nhachi Chademana was charged and refused bail.](https://web.archive.org/web/20240522064652/https://www.zimlive.com/crypto-couple-kidnapped-in-sa-and-smuggled-into-zimbabwe/) [(original link)](https://www.zimlive.com/crypto-couple-kidnapped-in-sa-and-smuggled-into-zimbabwe/) |
| June 2024 | Unidentified man | Berlin, Germany | [Nukri M., a 45-year-old Ukrainian lawyer, and accomplices allegedly planned from June 2024 to abduct a Berlin-Mitte businessman reputed to hold EUR 2 million in crypto, drag him into a vehicle and take him to a Polish apartment to force transfers, believing he would not report it. An undercover agent had infiltrated the group and the abduction never took place; Nukri M. was wanted since July 2024.](https://web.archive.org/web/20250826091127/https://www.tagesspiegel.de/berlin/prozess-nach-verdeckten-ermittlungen-angeklagter-soll-angriff-auf-kryptomillionar-in-berlin-geplant-haben-14085788.html) [(original link)](https://www.tagesspiegel.de/berlin/prozess-nach-verdeckten-ermittlungen-angeklagter-soll-angriff-auf-kryptomillionar-in-berlin-geplant-haben-14085788.html) |

| June 22 2024 | Liu Po-cheng | Taipei, Taiwan | [USDT trader Liu Po-cheng boarded a car near Taipei's Neihu tech park for a supposed sale when Lin Cheng-hsiang pointed a switchblade, blindfolded and tied him, the driver brandishing a second blade; impersonating him on his phone they had a contact transfer 21,084 USDT (~TWD 700,000) plus TWD 10,000 cash before dumping him in Shulin. Held captive for a day and a half, photographed naked under duress, the victim had his head split open against a steel bar (two stitches); more than five months after the complaint was filed, no suspect had been arrested, with the couple stating that the gang had already robbed one of their friends a week earlier.](https://web.archive.org/web/20260116114007/https://news.tvbs.com.tw/local/2931456) [(original link)](https://news.tvbs.com.tw/local/2931456) |
